# Skills · B · part 37

861 entries, blast-radius-drill to blender-impl-animation. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **blast-radius-drill**: Run the worst-case drill before an agent goes autonomous — the 'if this agent were fully hijacked right now, what's the damage' walk-through, the containment controls (caps, kill-switch, reversibility, isolation), and the recovery plan. Use when asked what's the worst my agent could do, run a blast-radius assessment, prepare for an agent going rogue, or am I ready to let this run unattended. Produces the worst-case walk-through, the containment controls, the reversibility audit, and the incident-recovery runbook. ([mohitagw15856/pm-claude-skills](https://github.com/mohitagw15856/pm-claude-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/pm-seatbelt/skills/blast-radius-drill) · ★1.6k · 3 copies)
- **blast-radius-estimator**: Helps estimate the blast radius when an AI agent skill turns malicious after widespread adoption. Analyzes inheritance chains, dependency graphs, and adoption trends to project how many agents could be affected. ([modbender/skill-library-mcp](https://github.com/modbender/skill-library-mcp/tree/HEAD/data/blast-radius-estimator) · ★21 · 7 copies)
- **blast-radius-grep**: The unit of change is the action or the fact, never the named list of places someone handed you. Before shipping a gate, constraint, rename, drop, or a correction to any duplicated, mirrored, or denormalised value, grep the whole repo — app, tests, seeds, scripts, open branches, and the built or rendered output — and fix every occurrence in the same pass; back a value copied into a second store with a trigger and a zero-rows drift-check query. Triggers on "change X in A and B" instructions, constraint or gate additions, drops and renames, denormalising a value, correcting any fact that is not… ([randommonicle/claude-skills](https://github.com/randommonicle/claude-skills/tree/HEAD/blast-radius-grep) · ★27)
- **blast-radius-guard**: Preflight consequential agent actions by resolving exact targets, authority, reversibility, retry ambiguity, rollback, and independent verification. Use before destructive, externally visible, bulk, financial, permission, deployment, send, or publish operations; do not use for read-only work or trivial reversible local edits. ([CHENG-LIANG1/badass-skills](https://github.com/CHENG-LIANG1/badass-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/blast-radius-guard))
- **blast-radius-permission**: 面向 OpenClaw 的风险半径权限设计技能。用于设计或审计删除、执行 shell、改配置、外部发送、子 agent 派生等高风险动作的权限与确认逻辑。 ([Richard0901/SKILL-warehouse](https://github.com/Richard0901/SKILL-warehouse/tree/HEAD/blast-radius-permission) · ★1)
- **blast-radius-pi**: Pi adaptation: Find what a change could break somewhere else before it ships, beyond the diff, and prove the one fact it's safe because of by running real code instead of writing it up. Use for 'blast radius of X', 'what could this break', or reviewing a small diff you don't trust. ([evoclock/pi-poteto-mode](https://github.com/evoclock/pi-poteto-mode/tree/HEAD/skills/blast-radius-pi) · ★9)
- **blast-radius-research**: &gt; **When to invoke**: Before implementing ANY proposed change, or before recommending solutions to any operational/architectural problem. This protocol replaces ad-hoc research prompts. &gt; &gt; **Why this exists**: Multiple failure modes observed across sessions (2026-04-21/22): &gt; - **Contradiction metadata**: Three passes needed because each asked a different question shape. Missed that skip-1-mentio ([juhinebhnani4/LDIP](https://github.com/juhinebhnani4/LDIP/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/blast-radius-research))
- **blast-radius-review**: Review a change by its blast radius, using Kin's graph to find what downstream code the change can reach. Use when reviewing a diff or pull request, deciding whether an edit is safe, or answering "what else breaks if I change this" in a repository admitted to Kin. ([kinlab-ai/kin](https://github.com/kinlab-ai/kin/tree/HEAD/plugins/kin-claude/skills/blast-radius-review) · ★68 · 2 copies)
- **blast-radius-rm-hold-unattended-subagent-stall**: A Bash call containing rm -rf or rm -f (test loop, staging script, cleanup) is held by a safety hook (e.g. Blast Radius) for 10 minutes and never runs, with 'no answer within 10 minutes' or a denial for an unresolved shell variable target. Use when writing any agent-authored script, test harness or cleanup step, or when dispatching a report-only subagent that may run shell commands. ([abhijitbansal/claude-skills](https://github.com/abhijitbansal/claude-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/core-workflow/skills/blast-radius-rm-hold-unattended-subagent-stall) · ★1)
- **blast-radius-scan**: Estimates change impact across code, runtime boundaries, and operational workflows. Use when a diff looks small but shared types, config, or data paths may spread the effect. ([SlipstreamAI/ClankerKit](https://github.com/SlipstreamAI/ClankerKit/tree/HEAD/skills/blast-radius-scan))
- **blast-radius-skill**: Find what a change could break beyond the diff and prove the one safety fact by running real code — 5-step evidence ladder. Triggers: blast radius, what could this break, pre-ship safety check. ([darellchua2/civiltekk-skills](https://github.com/darellchua2/civiltekk-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/blast-radius-skill) · ★7)

- **blast-radius-tracing**: Trace outward from changed symbols through callers and consumers, and identify the opaque edges where static tracing cannot follow — reflection, DI containers, event buses, feature flags. Use when working out what a code change can affect. ([Qualizeal/skills](https://github.com/Qualizeal/skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/qa-change-impact-analysis/skills/blast-radius-tracing))

([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/blast-score-ratio-allele-inference))

- **blast-scored-graph-path-walking**: Use when a metagenomic assembly graph (GFA from MetaCortex MC mode) contains unresolved branches from strain-level variation, and consensus contigs must be resolved into strain-specific sequences for per-strain validation. - **blastn-alignment-metrics**: Use when you need to quantify contiguous alignment proportion or sequence accuracy of long-read 16S consensus reads (e.g., INC-Seq or chopSeq output) against known reference sequences, especially to validate read-correction or size-selection steps. - **blastn-all-vs-all-genome-comparison**: Use when a set of viral genomes (e.g. CheckV-quality-filtered FASTAs from metagenomic assembly) must be dereplicated into species-level vOTUs, or when vOTU representatives need to be screened against external virome databases. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/blastn-database-scanning-profiling))

- **blastn-reference-genome-validation**: Use when a Kraken2-based taxonomic classification flags reads assigned to a user-defined species-of-interest watchlist taxid and those assignments need confirmation by blastn against user-selected reference genomes. - **blastn-sequence-homology-annotation**: Use when you have differentially expressed uTARs (Wilcoxon rank-sum, e.g. &gt;=25-50% of cluster cells expressing, &gt;=0.25-1 log-e fold change) and need gene-level or functional annotations from nucleotide sequence homology. - **blastradius**: Use when about to run, or when asked to review, a shell command that could reach beyond the current project - ssh, terraform, kubectl, helm, docker volume removal, cloud CLIs, database clients pointed at a remote host, force-push, systemd service changes, package removal, or any rm outside the working directory. ([coreyhiggins/blastradius](https://github.com/coreyhiggins/blastradius/tree/HEAD/skills/blastradius) · ★1)
- **blastradius**: Sizes the process a change earns to its blast radius rather than its diff size, so an isolated fix is not put through the same ceremony as a structural one. Default is heavy; light must be earned against a checkable list and stated out loud. Use when deciding how much review, planning or verification a change needs. ([pkloehn1/blastradius](https://github.com/pkloehn1/blastradius/tree/HEAD/skills/blastradius))

- **blazium-games-crash-reporting**: Send crash reports, standard launch events, and custom events from a game to Blazium Games. Use when the user wants crash reporting, crash dumps, symbols, launch health, error telemetry, or gameplay events for a Blazium Games project. ([blazium-games/games_skill](https://github.com/blazium-games/games_skill/tree/HEAD/skills/blazium-games-crash-reporting))

Use when the user asks to look at crashes, debug a crash, or triage crash reports for a Blazium Games project. ([blazium-games/games_skill](https://github.com/blazium-games/games_skill/tree/HEAD/skills/blazium-games-debug-crash))
- **blazium-games-deploy**: Ship game builds to Blazium Games from CI or the command line. - **blazium-games-get-started**: Connect Cursor to Blazium Games and route to the right skill. Blazium Games is a game publishing platform, with an infrastructure focus. Use when the user mentions Blazium Games for the first time, asks how to connect, or is unsure whether they want to play or publish. Use for peer RPC and scene replication only. Not LobbyClient, not WebRTCEnetSession, not ENetServer. Prefer JustAMCP networking_setup_multiplayer / networking_setup_rpc / networking_setup_sync. ([blazium-games/blazium-skills](https://github.com/blazium-games/blazium-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/blazium-multiplayer-core) · ★1)

- **blazium-physics**: Sets up Blazium 0.8.x / Godot 4.8.x collision layers, masks, Area2D/Area3D triggers, CharacterBody/RigidBody/StaticBody, and PhysicsDirectSpaceState ray / shape queries.
